Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE. (1) THE HEADLINE FINDING, WHICH OUTRANKS THE VERDICT: the table repair is NECESSARY AND NOT SUFFICIENT. Flipping the table does NOT reproduce cycle 8 restatement (compute 70,832.3 / bandwidth 59,073.7 / held out 660.9 / ratio 1.8263). It yields bandwidth 77,802.6 and ratio 1.6782, because population_overlap.py assigns populations BY EXCLUSION: COMPUTE_BOUND is the literal set {gemm_bf16, gemm_other, attention}, MIXED is {other}, and bandwidth_bound is everything else in CLASSES. So the four new classes all land in bandwidth by default -- 41,901.3 + 18,068.1 + 16,817.8 + 640.5 + 20.3 + 266.1 + 88.5 = 77,802.6 exactly -- which puts 18,068.1 us/step of compute-bound grouped-expert GEMM in the BANDWIDTH population, and stops holding out sort+loss because MIXED is now empty. The same by-exclusion rule produces the nonsense published rates gbps:loss 105,546.2 (105 TB/s, ~105x the card DRAM rate) and gbps:index_scatter_gather 2,209.7 (2.2x published). THEREFORE v2 STAYS OPT-IN AND THE DEFAULT IS NOT FLIPPED THIS TURN: shipping half the repair trades a known-wrong partition for a known-wrong population assignment. (2) The successor is named and is one act, not two: give each consumer population sets that REFUSE an unclassed class instead of defaulting -- population_overlap COMPUTE_BOUND gains gemm_grouped_expert and MIXED becomes {sort, loss, other}; captured_class_bandwidth COMPUTE_BOUND_BY_CONSTRUCTION and its adjudicated flag stop rating loss and sort as bandwidth -- and flip the v2 default in the same change, re-running every consumer. (3) captured_elementwise_bandwidth.py is COUPLED BY COPY: it restates the elementwise regex inline, commented as matched to captured_device_time_classes.py, so no repair of the shared table can reach it. Named, not counted in the denominator, not fixed. (4) It does not validate cycle 8 assignment. Gate (b) tests the TABLE against cycle 8, never cycle 8 against the world; the seven adjudications are taken as given. (5) It does not restate the three compositions that consume the old partition -- it measures that they WOULD move, which is not moving them. (6) It says nothing about the machine: 1.6782 is the same maximally-generous construction as 1.4726 and 1.8263, not a ceiling, with all three inflating assumptions intact. (7) One profiled trace, one geometry, one checkpoint, instrumentation dominating wall time, NO matched-run variation -- so nothing here satisfies the comparison-repeatability clause of the governed-run evidence contract.

ADJACENCY IS NECESSARY AND NOT SUFFICIENT and the verdict is worded to that: this does not prove one act collects both terms, it removes the ground for assuming two acts do. The composition needed the premise SUPPORTED, and merely-unrefuted is not enough for a ceiling that clears the arm bar by 0.08. THE CONSEQUENCE. Perfect collection of the whole 60.2 ms is 1.58x and every part of it is separately refuted already -- idle removal alone 1.266x, whole non-GEMM launch removal 3.88 ms, elementwise launch ceiling 2.65 ms -- so the composition was the only thing holding the non-GEMM route above the 1.5x bar. With its independence unsupported, NO ROUTE ON THIS CAMPAIGN CURRENTLY HAS A DEFENSIBLE CEILING ABOVE THE ARM BAR. That is a statement about which routes have been measured, not about the target, and it is exactly the condition under which the next act is a measurement of the one unbounded mechanism rather than an arm.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS REFUTATION DOES NOT CLOSE: (1) IT IS A PROFILED TRACE AND THE IDLE IS MOSTLY INSTRUMENTATION. Annotated span 503,890.3 us/step against a governed step of 163,760; device union 130,996.2 against the governed 131,055.6. So the union transfers and the SPAN does not -- roughly 91% of the 371,283 us interior idle is instrumentation. The SHARE is what is quoted and no absolute idle figure from this trace may be used as a governed quantity, per the separation clause of the governed-run evidence contract. (2) THE CLASS-FREQUENCY BIAS IS NARROWED, NOT REMOVED. A flush or serialization pause following a kernel is attributed to that kernel class, and the treatable classes are the numerous ones at every hole size. The minimum-gap control rules out the tiny-hole population as the driver; it cannot rule out that instrumentation inflates the share at all hole sizes. (3) TRANSFERRING THE SHARE TO THE GOVERNED STEP IS AN ASSUMPTION AND IS NOT MADE HERE. 1.46% of 32.7 ms would be about 0.5 ms of governed idle out of reach of an elementwise treatment; that arithmetic is [CANDIDATE] and is written here only so nobody else performs it silently. (4) IT DOES NOT REFUTE EITHER TERM ON ITS OWN. The elementwise class is 27,447.5 us/step of device union in this very trace and the idle is real; what is refuted is adding them. (5) IT DOES NOT PROVE ONE MECHANISM COLLECTS BOTH. Adjacency is consistent with a dependency stall or a memory-system wait that survives any consolidation. Measuring THAT needs dependency structure, which cycle 6 established this trace family cannot supply -- zero tensor-identity fields across its cpu_op events. (6) BYTE REDUCTION IS STILL THE ONE UNBOUNDED MECHANISM and is untouched by this cycle. Both bandwidth rows name it open and unsized: ~25.2 ms inside the elementwise class, ~40.6 ms across the non-GEMM path, including 5.18 ms of pure aten::copy_ doing no arithmetic. Its ceiling at PERFECT effectiveness over the whole elementwise class is 1.20x, which is below the arm bar by arithmetic -- so even the open mechanism does not clear the bar alone, and what is unmeasured is how much of it is real. (7) L2 RESIDENCY AND OCCUPANCY ARE STILL UNMEASURED, and three classes report above the published DRAM rate on a ~25 MB working set in a 72 MB L2. (8) ONE CAPTURE, ONE GEOMETRY, NO MATCHED-RUN VARIATION.

The hypothesis under test is that the non-arithmetic route set can reach 80,000 applied positions/s at this geometry; REFUTED means it cannot, at any effectiveness, and therefore that every remaining route lies in arithmetic time (precision at the GEMM sites, a cheaper algorithm, fewer FLOPs) or in positions per optimizer step, which is a CONTRACT change and the operator s decision. THIS BOUNDS THE ROUTE AND NOT THE EXIT. It is not an impossibility claim about the target, it is not rulable as one, and no artifact of this cycle may pre-build that case: measurement selects the route, never the exit, and impossibility is the operator s to rule from receipts.

No scheduling or fusion treatment deletes the multiply-accumulates the model owes per token; fusion absorbs OTHER work into them and makes none of them cheaper. THE HEADLINE DIRECTION IS NOT NEW AND THIS CYCLE DOES NOT CLAIM IT. rows-1-and-3-candidate-20260914.md section 6 already states in prose, as a [CANDIDATE CLAIM], that the bf16 GEMM class alone is 52.5 ms against a 51.2 ms terminal and that no treatment outside the GEMM class can reach it. THIS BOUNDS THE ROUTE AND NOT THE EXIT. It is not an impossibility claim about 80,000, it is not rulable as one, and nothing here pre-builds that case. What it selects: every remaining route acts on ARITHMETIC time -- lower-precision accumulation at the GEMM and grouped-expert sites, a cheaper algorithm, or fewer FLOPs -- or on positions per optimizer step, which is a LEARNING CONTRACT change and therefore the operator s decision, already raised at an honest ~1.1x and not to be re-nagged.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS REFUTATION DOES NOT CLOSE: (1) IT IS ONE GEOMETRY. 4,096 positions per update, CIA-3B, one checkpoint, one capture, no matched-run variation. The arithmetic floor is a property of THIS trainable set at THIS token count; a different active-expert count or sequence geometry moves every term in it. (2) IT SAYS NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER THE ARITHMETIC IS NECESSARY. 70.8 ms of measured GEMM and grouped-expert time is not a proof that those FLOPs must be issued. Fewer active experts, a cheaper attention, a sparser projection or a different algorithm all act inside the retained term and are untouched here. The bound says non-arithmetic treatments cannot reach the terminal; it does not say the arithmetic is irreducible. (3) PRECISION IS THE NAMED SURVIVING ROUTE AND IT IS NOT REFUTED. Cycle 17 refuted the 2x CONTRACTION-RATE premise at the one arm that has run, at 1.0597x and 1.2196x, and its own not_closed list keeps the route open: the contractions still BEAT bf16, the population beyond one projection site per geometry is unmeasured, the wrapper is unauthored and unsized, and FP16 accumulation carries the same published dense rate on Ada. What would settle it is a POPULATION-LEVEL contraction measurement across the 6.08 TF of linear work, which nothing has run. (4) THE PROFILED/GOVERNED TRANSFER IS AN ARGUMENT, NOT AN IDENTITY. The class unions come from a profiled capture and the step comes from a governed run. Cycle 18 justified the transfer for the UNION specifically -- 130,996.2 profiled against 131,055.6 governed, 0.05 percent -- and the SPAN does not transfer at all. If the union under instrumentation differed materially from the union without it, every term here moves. (5) THE ARITHMETIC/NON-ARITHMETIC SPLIT IS THE CLASS TABLE S. gemm_bf16 includes the cutlass memory-efficient attention kernels, and the nongemm bandwidth row already records that the attention class matched ZERO kernels in v2 because attention runs under names falling into gemm_bf16 or triton_fused. So some triton_fused time that is really attention arithmetic was REMOVED here rather than retained -- which makes the bound more generous, not less, and therefore does not weaken the refutation. It does mean the 70.8 ms floor is a LOWER bound on the arithmetic. (6) THE OPTIMIZER OVERLAP IS GRANTED, NOT SHOWN. The overlap row is INCONCLUSIVE with every not_closed line standing, and the measured timeline has zero concurrency. If that overlap is unachievable the best case is 16.8 ms worse, not better. (7) NO GATE MOVES AND NO TREATMENT IS NAMED. This is route selection, not an arm. The attention block aggregates describe a homogeneous call populationdiagnostic, existing capture24 forward and 24 backward kernel instances per step are not 24 repetitions of one call.The criterion required max-over-median at or below 1.50, top-quartile share at or below 0.40 (against the 0.25 a uniform population gives), and a per-window mean spread at or below 1.20.SUPPORTEDCYCLE 21 UPDATE -- what a SUPPORTED homogeneity result does NOT close.
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
Homogeneity fails in EITHER direction on ANY of three legs, and the verdict names which leg fired. (a) SPREAD: max over median above 1.50 -- one or more instances cost half again the typical call. (b) CONCENTRATION: the top quartile of instances carries more than 0.40 of the direction s total time, against the 0.25 a uniform population gives. (c) DRIFT: the ratio of the largest to the smallest per-window mean above 1.20, which would mean the steps differ from each other rather than the calls differing within a step -- a different finding from (a) and (b) and reported as such. WHAT REFUTED WOULD MEAN. That the attention block s per-step aggregates -- including the 64.0 TF/s achieved rate and the 3.805x backward/forward ratio -- describe a mixture rather than a call, and that any treatment sized against the class must first name which sub-population it acts on. It would NOT refute the rate measurement itself, which is an aggregate over the same time either way, and it would say nothing about why the spread exists. Bounds the ROUTE, not the exit.

WHAT THIS LICENSES, WHICH IS THE POINT OF THE CYCLE. The attention block s per-step aggregates describe a CALL and not a mixture, so the 3.8053x backward-over-forward TIME ratio against a 2.0-2.5x FLOP ratio is a property of every one of those 24 calls rather than an average over heterogeneous geometries. The successor hypothesis the parent row registered -- attention-backward-carries-the-distance -- therefore cannot be answered by finding a sub-population, and the obvious sub-kernel framing was already dead before this cycle opened (the backward is one fused symbol, 144 launches, no separable term). WHAT IT DOES NOT DO. It explains nothing about WHY the backward sits where it does, it names no treatment, it composes no ceiling, and it does not revisit the 64.0 TF/s achieved rate, which is an aggregate over the same time either way. A SUPPORTED homogeneity result is a statement about the EVIDENCE -- that a per-step figure may be reasoned with as a per-call figure -- and not a statement about the route.
Limitations, and what this does not close
CYCLE 21 UPDATE -- what a SUPPORTED homogeneity result does NOT close. (1) IT ANSWERS NOTHING ABOUT WHY. The backward is 3.8053x the forward in time against a 2.0-2.5x FLOP ratio, and this cycle establishes only that the gap is uniform across calls. Traffic, occupancy, and the recompute question (whether this cutlass backward recomputes S = QK^T, which the parent row carries as an unverified 4-vs-5-matmul range) are all untouched, and no instrument on this campaign produces measured traffic or measured occupancy. (2) HOMOGENEITY WITHIN ONE TRACE IS NOT HOMOGENEITY IN GENERAL. Six windows of one profiled capture at one geometry, B=4 S=1024 H=16 D=64, all documents the same length. A batch with ragged document lengths would present different causal geometries to a block-causal kernel and this result says nothing about that case. The governed hour is exactly such a case and is unmeasured here. (3) IT IS A PROFILED TRACE. No matched-run variation, and the per-instance durations carry whatever per-launch cost the profiler imposes. The tightness of the distribution makes an instrumentation artefact less likely to be hiding a spread, but it does not measure the artefact. This cycle measures what is left of that join once the execution parameter is compared rather than the shape: the arm s m4096 configuration decomposes 4096 rows into 16 groups of 256, the production site decomposes the same 4096 rows into 4, and the arm s admissible row-block padding is 23.07 percent against the site s 4.61. Structural coverage 0.0000 of the class. WHAT THIS DOES NOT SAY. It does not say the 1.0597x would be different at G=4. It does not say the ratio depends on G at all. Whether a ratio measured at one group structure transfers to another is a question about the kernel and is not answered by two receipts; what IS answered is that the transfer has never been checked and that every published FP8 figure on this campaign rests on it. The 4.61 and 23.07 percent figures are UPPER BOUNDS on padding waste from 64-row block rounding, not measured waste, and they are reported as the reason a mismatch could matter rather than as the size of the error. AND IT DOES NOT MOVE A GATE. #1945 composite 10 percent, gate A UNMET at 23,380.183126331838 of 80,000.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE. (1) THE PRODUCTION PER-GROUP ROW COUNTS ARE STILL UNKNOWN AND ARE NOT ON DISK. The group COUNT is 4, confirmed twice. The partition of 4096 rows across those 4 groups is runtime routing data that appears in no trace, receipt or custody -- a filesystem search for group_sizes, expert_counts, rows_per_expert, tokens_per_expert and assignment_counts returns nothing. Only the mean, 1024, is derivable, and a mean is not a distribution. (2) NOTHING MEASURES WHETHER THE FP8 RATIO DEPENDS ON G. This cycle establishes a structural mismatch; it does not price it. The kernel rounds each group to a 64-row block and each program exits when its first row is past its group end, so G plausibly changes the ratio -- plausibly is not a measurement, and the sign is not predicted here. (3) THE OTHER THREE-QUARTERS OF THE CLASS IS UNTOUCHED BY ANY FP8 MEASUREMENT IN ANY CONFIGURATION. The second forward site [4096,3072] x [4,3072,1024] is 1,951.0 us/step and the two backward grad populations are 8,200.3 and 4,074.0 us/step. The arm ran at neither direction s production geometry. (4) THE DENSE PATH STILL HAS NO FP8 MEASUREMENT AT ALL -- 38,260.2 us/step of aten::mm, ten dense symbols, zero arms ever run against them. Unchanged from cycle 22 and not addressed here. (5) BACKWARD SITES ARE EXCLUDED BY CONSTRUCTION. Their recorded geometry does not carry the grouped weight operand, so this instrument cannot read their G. Whether the arm s backward configuration matches theirs is unmeasured, not shown to differ. (6) ONE PROFILED RECEIPT ON EACH SIDE, NO MATCHED-RUN VARIATION. Nothing here may be quoted as an improvement or a ceiling under the governed-run evidence contract, and this row claims neither. (7) CYCLE 22 S SEVENTH LINE STANDS UNAUDITED: whether any OTHER published composition on this campaign joins a measurement to a population or a configuration it was not measured at has still never been censused. This cycle is the second instance found by looking at one join, which is weak evidence that looking at the rest would find more, and no evidence that it would not.

Result in full
The frozen criterion listed five independent ways to refute and required all five to fail. (a) G is bound to 4 by an executable refusal, cross-checked against the slot vector -- not refuted. (b) Row-to-group assignment is whole-chunk: one elected winner per chunk expanded by repeat_interleave, so a chunk cannot split across groups -- not refuted. (c) The chunk size is the constant 256 and the measured geometry is four documents of 1024, so every chunk is exactly 256 rows -- not refuted. (d) Every admissible group row count is 256 times a chunk count and therefore a multiple of the 64-row block; actual padding across the whole space is 0 rows -- not refuted. (e) The grid (cdiv(m,64), cdiv(n,128), groups) and the early-exit predicate were both read from the launch site and the kernel body, and the receipt independently records the measured op as DynamicGrouped -- not refuted. SUPPORTED. WHAT THIS UNBLOCKS, WHICH IS THE POINT OF THE CYCLE. Cycle 23 could not name a production partition to author an arm at, because the per-group ROW COUNTS are runtime data on no artifact. They are still on no artifact -- and they no longer need to be. The source bounds the space to 969 partitions of a known shape, and the balanced one, four groups of 1024 rows, is a member. An FP8 arm at a production-admissible structure is authorable today. AND IT RETIRES CYCLE 23 PADDING FRAMING WHILE LEAVING ITS VERDICT STANDING. Cycle 23 reported admissible padding upper bounds of 0.0461 for the production site and 0.2307 for the arm, and said explicitly they were upper bounds and not measured waste. They are now computed: BOTH ARE ZERO, because 256 divides 64 evenly on both sides. The padding channel of the structural mismatch is dead. What survives, and is stronger, is the LAUNCH channel: identical useful work (1,536 working programs either way, which is why the efficiency receipt could call forward FLOPs routing-independent) against 4.00x the launched programs and 5.0x the programs that launch and immediately exit. Cycle 23 verdict -- no FP8 measurement at a structure production runs, structural coverage 0.0 of 18,068.1 us/step -- is untouched. NO TIMING CLAIM. This cycle prices nothing. It does not say the 1.0597x would differ at G=4, does not predict a sign, and quotes no ceiling. It says what an arm may now be authored at. NO GATE MOVES. Composite 10 percent, gate A UNMET at 23,380.183126331838 of 80,000.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE. (1) THE ACTUAL PRODUCTION PARTITION IS STILL UNKNOWN -- only its SPACE is. Which of the 969 compositions occurs at any given step is routing data that appears in no artifact, and nothing here records the observed distribution over that space. An arm can now be authored at a MEMBER of the space; it cannot yet be authored at the TYPICAL member, because nothing measures typicality. The balanced 4x1024 case is the mean by arithmetic and a mean is still not a mode. (2) NOTHING MEASURES WHETHER THE FP8 RATIO DEPENDS ON G. Unchanged from cycle 23 and the whole reason the arm is owed. The launch-count factor of 4 is a derived program count, not a time. (3) THE BACKWARD WEIGHT KERNEL IS NOT ANALYSED HERE. Its grid is also linear in the group count and its output tensor is [G,K,N], so a 16-group call writes four times the weight-gradient bytes -- derivable but not derived, and the backward sites carry no grouped weight operand in their recorded geometry, so they were excluded by construction in cycle 23 and again here. (4) A RAGGED GEOMETRY WOULD CHANGE THE ALIGNMENT RESULT. The zero-padding finding holds for the measured four-documents-of-1024 geometry, where every chunk is exactly 256. A document length that is not a multiple of 256 produces a short tail chunk and the instrument refuses rather than generalising -- so nothing here says production pads zero at every geometry it could run. (5) THREE QUARTERS OF THE GROUPED-EXPERT CLASS STILL HAS NO FP8 MEASUREMENT IN ANY CONFIGURATION (second forward site 1,951.0 us/step, backward grads 8,200.3 and 4,074.0), and THE DENSE PATH STILL HAS NONE AT ALL (38,260.2 us/step of aten::mm). Carried from cycles 22 and 23, untouched. (6) THIS IS A SOURCE READ, NOT A RUN. Every fact is anchored to a line at head 20338ddc and refuses when the anchor is absent, which makes it checkable -- it does not make it an observation of the executing kernel. No profile confirms the launched-program counts. (7) CYCLE 22 SEVENTH LINE STILL STANDS UNAUDITED: whether any OTHER published composition on this campaign joins a measurement to a population or a configuration it was not measured at. SUPPORTED. The frozen criterion refuted the hypothesis if ANY of four held; all four failed, each at an anchored line in bytes digest-pinned to the run own binding. (a) THE OFFSETS VECTOR IS NOT BUILT FROM lengths. The worker builds offsets as torch.tensor([m]) -- a ONE-element literal, counted by the instrument rather than assumed -- and no line in the worker binds lengths and offsets together. The `lengths` list is consumed only to build ends as a cumsum of lengths padded to sixteen, passed with NC=16: the dW reduction boundaries, not a grouping. (b) EVERY GROUPED LAUNCH PASSES LITERAL 1 ON THE GROUP AXIS. The worker weight kernel and all three candidate-prototype kernels (forward, dx, dw). The instrument reads each axis as an integer and refuses if it disagrees with the offsets length or the weight operand -- proved by a red where a four-group worker is READ as four and then refuses on the disagreement, so the reader is not wired to the answer. (c) THE WEIGHT OPERAND IS UNSQUEEZED TO [1,k,n] on both paths (weight.T.unsqueeze(0) and w8.T.unsqueeze(0)). This is the load-bearing one: rows() at 514a212f derives its group count as `groups, bk, n = b.shape` and requires offsets.shape == (groups,), so the group count IS the weight leading dimension. (d) THE BYTES ARE THE BYTES THAT RAN: three digests matched the run binding exactly. CORROBORATION THE CRITERION DID NOT REQUIRE: the baseline path launches NO grouped kernel at all -- it is dense scaled matmuls that iterate `lengths` as dW partial boundaries, which is independently what the field is for. And the fourth traced label, control, is literally the native function again (lambda: native(control)), so all four traced paths run one group or none. THEREFORE THE CORRECTED JOIN, and it inverts what cycle 24 published. At the forward site [4096,1024] x [4,1024,3072], 3,842.7 us/step: the arm at G=1 launches 1,536 programs and ALL 1,536 do work, ZERO empty. Production at G=4 launches 6,144 and the same 1,536 do work -- 4,608 empty, 75 percent of the grid. LAUNCHED 0.25x, not 4.00x. Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THE REFUTATION OF MY OWN TWO CYCLES DOES NOT CLOSE. 1. Sizing dim0 per group is a source-derived candidate treatment never before named. Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THE REFUTATION DOES NOT CLOSE. (1) THE LARGEST BUCKET IS NOT CLEARED BY THE INSTRUMENT. SINGLE_PROVENANCE means ONE source token appeared in the +-260 character window, which is a property of the SENTENCE and not of the operands. 685 of 1,126 compositions are in it. A composition joining two runs in a sentence naming one receipt buckets SINGLE_PROVENANCE and nothing in the first instrument clears it. The literal-reuse sweep was built and run to address exactly that gap and it is a RANKING, not a clearing: it finds shared numbers under disjoint sources and cannot see two operands that never share a literal. (2) THE EXTRACTOR OVER-FLAGS IN TWO KNOWN WAYS. It calls a sentence CROSS_PROVENANCE when it names a receipt AND the trace that receipt derives from -- a chain, not a cross. And it matches the word "ceiling" inside a sentence that denies a ceiling, which is how three PROPOSED rows with no measurement at all entered the census. Both are properties of a pattern extractor and both inflate the flagged set rather than shrink it. (3) IT IS A CENSUS OF TWO RECORDS, NOT OF EVERY PUBLISHED SURFACE. A composition that was published in the ledger and never registered is outside this enumeration entirely, and the ledger is the larger prose surface by a wide margin. (4) THE GATED-SOURCE ARGUMENT IS A SOURCE READ, NOT A MEASUREMENT. Both dissolutions above rest on reading that the step-path delta between two commits is inert with the treatment off. No control run at the later commit with the treatment off exists on either pair, so the inertness is shown from the source text and not from the card. That is the strongest evidence available without spending two arms, and it is weaker than a measurement. (5) A WELL-CONDITIONED JOIN IS STILL A JOIN. Every one of the 20 declares its cross-provenance; none of them is thereby made commensurable. The declaration means the number may not be quoted BARE, and this campaign has already published bare quotations of conditioned numbers -- which is a different defect, in the QUOTING rather than in the row, and this cycle did not look for it. THE SUCCESSOR THAT FOLLOWS FROM (3) AND (5): census the PROSE surfaces -- the push ledger first, at roughly 10,700 lines the largest -- for quotations of registry numbers stripped of the condition the registry row attaches to them. That is the same hypothesis pointed at the place the record is actually read, and it is analysis-only.

The unit-fences-all-literals rule is a declared design decision and this is its cost. (2) THE QUOTATION CARRIES ITS FENCE IN VOCABULARY THE FENCE TOKEN SET DOES NOT ENUMERATE -- the 1.4726 group, 1,581.5 and 1,987.0. The ledger says "BELOW the 1.5x arm bar. All three assumptions behind that number were chosen to INFLATE it"; the receipt says "Neither is close to the 1.5x arm bar, and neither is a ceiling on any treatment". Both are fences; neither matches "not a ceiling". (3) THE OCCURRENCE IS A MENTION OR A DELTA-TABLE ENTRY, NOT A USE -- "ratio 1.4726 -> 1.6782" in a consumer-delta table, "resolving it changes every composition, including the 1.4726 figure". THE FENCED BUCKET WAS AUDITED TOO, because an over-inclusive fence rule is the only direction that could hide a violation behind a zero. Seven of the 40 were absorbed by a single weak token (profiled, instrumentation, conditional, no ceiling) and all seven were read: each window explicitly refuses the forbidden use in its own sentence -- "expressing it against the 175,190 us governed step would compose a profiled quantity with a governed one, and none is quoted"; "NOT an impossibility claim and not a statement about the target"; "it does not say the 1.0597x would differ at G=4, does not predict a sign, and quotes no ceiling". THE 12 SUPERSEDED ARE GENUINE and one of them is the whole point: the 80,000 impossibility sentence, withdrawn in all three places it lived. That is this defect class occurring once, in the governing rule rather than in a prose quotation, and being caught and corrected -- which is what the criterion means by a superseded quotation not counting. WHAT THE NULL RESULT ACTUALLY FOUND: the campaign fences numbers at the point of QUOTATION, not only at the point of measurement, and it does so in a vocabulary far wider than any pattern set I would have written. The fence travels with the number.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE, and the successor. (1) THE CENSUS IS OF LITERALS UNDER EXPLICIT PROHIBITIONS, which is 21 numbers out of the hundreds this campaign publishes. A number whose row states a CONDITION rather than a PROHIBITION is outside it by design, and that is the larger population. (2) THE UNIT-FENCES-ALL-LITERALS RULE PRODUCED 22 OF THE 48 FLAGS and would equally produce false NEGATIVES in the other direction: a prohibition whose literal sits two sentences away is not extracted at all. Neither direction is measured. (3) THE FENCE TOKEN SET IS PROVABLY NARROWER THAN THE PROSE. The adjudication found at least four fencing formulations it does not carry. Widening it would shrink the candidate set and would also make the instrument easier to satisfy, which is the wrong direction for a detector whose null result is the finding. (4) THE SURFACES ARE THE THREE ON DISK. The issue body, its comments and the operator-facing chat lines are not censused, and the chat lines are the surface with the most compression and therefore the highest prior for this defect. They are not durably recorded anywhere this instrument can read, which is itself worth naming. (5) A QUOTATION CAN VIOLATE A FENCE WITHOUT REUSING THE LITERAL -- rounded, rescaled, or restated in words. This census is literal-matched and blind to all of it. (6) NOTHING HERE RETIRES THE CARE. Two consecutive null results on the record do not license quoting a conditioned number bare; they are evidence that not doing so has been working. THE SUCCESSOR, and it follows from (1) and (5): the three cheap analysis-only cycles in a row have each returned a null on the record and none has moved a gate. SUPPORTED requires the G=4 forward to be within that same measured floor of G=1 -- the empty program is free at the production partition, and the 1.0597x FP8 ratio's BF16 denominator transfers to G=4 unchanged on the forward. INCONCLUSIVE is reserved for a run that did not discriminate: a floor as large as or larger than the G=4 minus G=1 difference, a refused capture, or a failed identity check. It is NOT available for a result whose sign is unwelcome. THE RUN REFUSES RATHER THAN REPORTS if the G=4 forward output is not BITWISE EQUAL to the G=1 forward output, or the G=4 dX not bitwise equal to the G=1 dX. Non-identity means the two configurations did not compute the same arithmetic and no timing difference between them is attributable to geometry. Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE, and the one thing in it that corrected a reading of mine before the reading reached an artifact. (1) THE dW RESULT WAS CONFOUNDED AND THE CONFOUND WAS DECISIVE. My working mechanism reading was occupancy: the G=1 _weights grid (16,24,1) is 384 programs on a 128-SM card against G=4's 1,536. A second leg run under the same window refutes it. NC -- the per-group chunk count, a kernel constexpr -- moved with G in the arm: 16 at G=1 against 4 at G=4. A 2x2 over G in {1,4} and NC in {4,16} separates them: g1_nc16 190.614, g1_nc4 166.359, g4_nc4 174.592, g4_nc16 212.451 us. The GRID term is POSITIVE in both directions (+8.233 us at NC=4, +21.837 us at NC=16): holding chunking fixed, MORE groups costs MORE, which is the direction the extra output bytes predict and the opposite of the occupancy reading. The whole apparent win is the CHUNK-LOOP term (-24.255 us at G=1, -37.859 us at G=4): fewer, larger chunks are faster. The interaction is -13.604 us, comparable to the terms themselves, so the two do not separate additively and the receipt says so rather than hiding it. The consequence that matters: at MATCHED chunking, production (g4_nc4, 174.592) is SLOWER than one group (g1_nc4, 166.359) by 8.233 us on dW. Every group contracted against the same [1024,3072] slab, replicated with .repeat() into four distinct slabs rather than an expand view so no cache line is shared. The elementwise class is l2 resident not dram bounddiagnostic, existing captureAn elementwise kernel whose operands were just written by the preceding kernel, and whose per-invocation working set fits in the 72 MB L2, can be served entirely from L2; its traffic never reaches…The frozen criterion was: REFUTED IF more than 50 percent of the elementwise class's sized device time is spent in kernel invocations whose per-invocation working set EXCEEDS 72 MB.SUPPORTEDThe elementwise row's not_closed line stands untouched: capacity is necessary and not sufficient, nothing here reads a cache counter, and 87 percent of class time being L2-resident-CAPABLE says nothing about whether a…
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
MORE THAN 50 PERCENT of the elementwise class's sized device time is spent in kernel invocations whose per-invocation working set EXCEEDS 72 MB. Such an invocation cannot be L2-resident by capacity alone, so if the bulk of the class sits there, the class genuinely touches DRAM, the aggregate excess is an artifact of a minority of small kernels, and the byte-reduction direction survives at something near its stated size.
Instrument
A bucketing pass over the elementwise population of an on-disk captured trace, reusing the join and the byte derivation of scripts/issue1945/captured_elementwise_bandwidth.py: for each sized elementwise kernel, compute the per-invocation working-set bytes already derived there, bucket its device time by that figure against the 72 MB L2 capacity, and report the time share on each side plus the distribution. Analysis-only: no GPU, no lease, no new capture. NOTE OF RECORD -- the trace the 1,160.6 GB/s figure came from (cia-captured-trace-20260914T172624Z.json) is no longer on disk; this runs against cia-captured-trace-20260914T182441Z.json, a DIFFERENT capture. The class totals may therefore differ from the source receipt, and whether the aggregate rate reproduces at all on an independent capture is a second, unplanned reading this run produces.
Evidence
state/issue1945-receipts/elementwise-l2-residency-20260915T0335Z.json
Result in full
The frozen criterion was: REFUTED IF more than 50 percent of the elementwise class's sized device time is spent in kernel invocations whose per-invocation working set EXCEEDS 72 MB. It is 12.88 percent, across 9 kernels per step, at sized coverage 0.996. The criterion is not met, so the reading is SUPPORTED exactly as frozen. AND THE MECHANISM THE HYPOTHESIS PROPOSED IS REFUSED BY THE SAME DATA, which is reported here rather than left in the receipt. The explanation offered was that the above-DRAM aggregate is an L2 effect. If it were, the population that CANNOT be L2-resident by capacity would sit at or below the 1008 GB/s reference. It sits furthest above it: exceeds-L2 achieves 1390.9 GB/s against fits-L2 at 1146.8, and the >256MB bucket 1560.3. The likelier cause is an instrument property, not a cache one -- the byte model charges every sized input read once plus the largest written once, which over-counts aten::fill_ two-for-one and aten::copy_ three-for-two, and those two sites are 2,408.6 of the 3,522.1 us in the exceeding bucket. So the capacity question is answered and the residency question is not, and the aggregate excess over the published reference is at least partly an artifact of the byte model.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THE REFUTED MECHANISM DOES NOT CLOSE. (a) L2 RESIDENCY IS STILL UNMEASURED. The elementwise row's not_closed line stands untouched: capacity is necessary and not sufficient, nothing here reads a cache counter, and 87 percent of class time being L2-resident-CAPABLE says nothing about whether a buffer survived between its producer and its consumer. What changed is that the above-DRAM aggregate is no longer usable as indirect evidence for residency, because the non-resident population exceeds the reference by more. (b) THE BYTE MODEL IS NOW THE LOAD-BEARING SUSPECT AND IS ITSELF UNMEASURED. Both this receipt and the one it extends derive bytes from recorded input dims, and the over-count is now known to be large enough to matter at the top of the distribution. A measured DRAM-traffic counter would settle it and this build has none. (c) THE BYTE-REDUCTION DIRECTION IS NEITHER CONFIRMED NOR REFUTED. It was the reason this cycle ran; the cycle failed to price it, because pricing it needs the residency answer the mechanism failure just removed the shortcut to. THE UNPLANNED FINDING, which is the cycle's real product: the class is BIMODAL. 1,844 of 3,336 sized kernels per step have working sets at or under 1 MB and take 2,269.4 us -- 8.3 percent of class time -- at 29.4 GB/s, 2.9 percent of the reference. Kernels between 8 MB and 72 MB are 1,004 per step and take 20,519.7 us, 75.1 percent of class time, at 1,202-1,261 GB/s. The bandwidth-bound ruling describes the second population and not the first. The small tail is the launch/occupancy population the row-1 framing was about, and it is 2,269.4 us: 1.4 percent of a 163,760 us governed step, far below the 1.5x arm bar, so it names which milliseconds belong to which story rather than nominating an arm. INSTRUMENT DEFECT FOUND AND CURED IN-CYCLE. The first run returned every kernel unsized -- that capture was taken without record_shapes -- and the script printed SUPPORTED, because an empty sized population makes the exceeding share 0.0 and the "more than 50 percent" criterion reads as unsatisfied. A verdict from an empty denominator is the persistent-lock-file defect inside a measurement. A coverage floor now refuses below 0.50 and names the cause; the refusal is receipted at elementwise-l2-residency-REFUSAL-20260915T0332Z.json, exit 2.

The elementwise byte model overcounts fill and copydiagnostic, existing captureMECHANISM: the elementwise class is not achieving 1.17x the published DRAM reference.The frozen criterion was: REFUTED IF, after charging aten::fill_ for ONE buffer rather than two and aten::copy_ for TWO rather than three with every other op unchanged, the elementwise class aggregate achieved rate remains AT OR ABOVE the 1008 GB/s published…REFUTEDWHAT THE REFUTATION DOES NOT CLOSE, and the first item inverts a ruling I made four hours ago.
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
REFUTED IF, after correcting the byte charge at the two named sites -- aten::fill_ charged the bytes of ONE buffer rather than two, aten::copy_ charged TWO rather than three, every other op unchanged -- the elementwise class aggregate achieved rate on trace cia-captured-trace-20260914T183257Z.json remains AT OR ABOVE the 1008 GB/s published DRAM reference. That would mean the over-count is not what puts the class above the reference and some other cause is in play.
Instrument
Analysis-only, no card, no lease, no new capture: re-run the elementwise join of captured_elementwise_bandwidth.py over the trace already on disk with op_bytes() replaced by a per-op byte model that charges the true buffer count at aten::fill_ and aten::copy_ and is unchanged everywhere else, then report the class aggregate both ways side by side plus the per-site deltas. The uncorrected figure is kept in the receipt so the correction is auditable rather than a replacement.
Evidence
state/issue1945-receipts/elementwise-byte-model-correction-20260915T0353Z.json
Result in full
The frozen criterion was: REFUTED IF, after charging aten::fill_ for ONE buffer rather than two and aten::copy_ for TWO rather than three with every other op unchanged, the elementwise class aggregate achieved rate remains AT OR ABOVE the 1008 GB/s published DRAM reference. It remains above. Incumbent 1185.2 GB/s (1.176 of the reference); corrected 1079.9 GB/s (1.071). The correction removes 2,861,568,527 bytes per step -- 8.89 percent of the charged total, worth 105.3 GB/s -- and the class is still above the reference. REFUTED. So the over-count is REAL and is NOT the explanation. It was the leading suspect because the two ops dominate the top of the rate distribution, and it accounts for less than half the excess over the reference. Sized coverage 0.9900 of 27,447.4 us/step across 3,447 kernels; the coverage refusal was proved as a deliberate red against a capture taken without record_shapes before this reading was taken, exit 2 at coverage 0.0000.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THE REFUTATION DOES NOT CLOSE, and the first item inverts a ruling I made four hours ago. (a) THE PER-SITE TABLE REINSTATES THE L2 EXPLANATION CYCLE 30 REFUSED, and that refusal was taken on aggregates the over-count dominated. Cycle 30 argued that residency cannot be lifting the rate because the population that CANNOT fit in the 72 MB L2 achieved 1390.9 GB/s against the fitting population 1146.8. The two largest members of that exceeding bucket are exactly the two mis-charged ops, and correcting them moves copy_ [4096,32768] from 1480.6 to 1184.4 GB/s and fill_ [4096,32768] from 1803.1 to 901.5 -- the latter now BELOW the reference. Meanwhile the sites that stay furthest above it after correction are small: fill_ [16793600] at 1995.0, copy_ [4096,4,4,64] at 2253.1, fill_ [4096,1024] at 2021.4, all working sets of 34 MB or less and comfortably L2-resident. That is the shape residency predicts and the opposite of what cycle 30 measured. It is an OBSERVATION from this cycle per-site output, not a ruling: the corrected residency split has not been recomputed, and until it is, cycle 30 verdict stands as published with this doubt recorded against it. Successor: the-corrected-byte-model-reverses-the-l2-residency-refusal-20260915. (b) IT MEASURES NO BYTES. Both figures are counts derived from recorded input dims. No device-traffic counter exists in this build, which is why the estimate was load-bearing at all, and the governed-run evidence contract requires measured traffic reported separately from estimated traffic. (c) THE CORRECTION IS TO TWO OPS. Every other op keeps the generic sum-plus-max charge, which still treats the largest input as the output for reduction-shaped ops and still models no cache reuse in either direction. (d) IT RULES NOTHING ABOUT WHETHER THE CLASS IS BANDWIDTH-BOUND. A class above the published reference is consistent with genuine cache reuse and with a residual over-count elsewhere in the model; nothing here discriminates, and achieved occupancy would. (e) THE INCUMBENT FIGURE DISAGREES SLIGHTLY WITH ITS SIBLING: 1185.2 GB/s here against 1178.3 from the residency instrument on the same trace, because the two scripts admit slightly different sized populations (coverage 0.9900 against 0.996). Under one percent and it changes no verdict, but two instruments reporting different numbers for the same quantity is a defect and is recorded rather than rounded away. THE CONSEQUENCE FOR THE ONLY ARM ABOVE THE BAR, stated as what it is. Cycle 31 found exactly one treatment clearing the 1.5x bar, and its larger half is the whole elementwise class removed as device time, 27,500 us. This cycle leaves that class reading above the card published streaming reference even after a real correction. If that reading is traffic rather than artifact, fusing the class removes launches and intermediates but not the bytes, and the lever is much smaller than its class size -- which would leave the campaign with NO measured arm above the bar. That is a consequence worth acting on and it is NOT established here: it turns on (a), which is open.

Result in full
The frozen criterion reads: REFUTED IF the exceeding-L2 achieved rate REMAINS AT OR ABOVE the fitting-L2 achieved rate under the corrected byte model, with the corrected charge used for both bucket membership and rate. It does not. Incumbent 1390.9 exceeding against 1146.8 fitting; corrected 1063.7 exceeding against 1075.0 fitting. The ordering INVERTS, so the criterion is not met and the hypothesis is SUPPORTED. AND THE MARGIN IS 11.3 GB/s ON FIGURES NEAR 1070, ABOUT 1.05 PERCENT, WITH NO BETWEEN-RUN FLOOR AND NO MATCHED-RUN VARIATION. A one-percent inversion from a two-op correction to an estimated byte model is not a measurement of the cache. What the verdict licenses is narrow and is stated as such: cycle 30 refused the residency mechanism BECAUSE the ordering ran the wrong way, and that reason does not survive the correction. It does not establish residency, and cycle 30 conclusion is withdrawn as UNSUPPORTED rather than replaced by its opposite. HALF OF MY OWN PREDICTED MECHANISM IS REFUTED INSIDE THIS SUPPORTED VERDICT AND IS RECORDED RATHER THAN ABSORBED. The explain stage named two compounding effects, rate and membership. MEMBERSHIP DID ESSENTIALLY NOTHING: exactly one kernel changed side, aten::fill_ [32768,1024] at 52.3 us/step, 0.19 percent of sized class time. The inversion is the rate half alone. THE READING THAT MATTERS TO THE CAMPAIGN, and it cuts against the arm rather than for it: under the corrected model the L2-CAPABLE population reads 1075.0 GB/s, which is 1.066 of the 1008 GB/s published reference, and the aggregate is 1073.6, also 1.066. Both sides are above the reference. So the class being above the reference is NOT explained by residency either -- the correction removed the inverted ordering, not the excess.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE, and the first line is the successor because it changes the size of the only above-bar arm. THE ELEMENTWISE CLASS IS NOT ONE POPULATION AND THE SPLIT THAT MATTERS IS NOT THE L2 ONE. The corrected size distribution shows 1,844 kernels per step under 1 MB carrying 2,269.4 us at 28.1 GB/s -- 2.8 percent of the published reference. That subpopulation is not bandwidth-bound by any reading; it is launch-bound, and it is the part of the class a fusion treatment can actually collect. The other roughly 25,000 us sits at or above the reference. Successor: the-elementwise-class-splits-into-a-launch-bound-tail-and-a-streaming-body-20260915, which sizes the collectable half properly. NOTE ON WHO COMPOSES IT: turning those two figures into a revised ceiling for the 1.58x stack is a CANDIDATE claim, not a measurement, and my own record says that is where I fail. The successor reports the two populations as quantities; any ceiling drawn from them states its mechanism premise separately and carries the self-check. CYCLE 30 VERDICT IS WITHDRAWN, NOT REVERSED. Its refusal rested on an ordering produced by an inflated numerator. Withdrawing the reason leaves the residency question OPEN, not answered. Nothing here shows bytes were in L2; a working set at or below 72 MB is capable of residency and that is all the bucket means. THE INVERSION IS ONE PERCENT AND UNREPEATED. One capture, one process, under profiling instrumentation, no matched-run variation. Under the governed-run evidence contract that is not enough to claim an ordering in either direction, and a future correction of comparable size anywhere in the model could move it back. IT STILL MEASURES NO BYTES. Both models are counts from recorded input dims; this build carries no memory-traffic counter and no achieved-occupancy counter. The one instrument that would discriminate a bandwidth-bound class from a modelling residue does not exist on this host, and both halves of this campaign bandwidth story rest on estimates labelled as such. TWO INSTRUMENTS STILL DISAGREE BY UNDER ONE PERCENT on the class aggregate: 1073.6 GB/s here against 1079.9 from the cycle-32 instrument on the same trace, from admitted populations differing in coverage (0.996 against 0.9900). It changes no verdict and it is recorded rather than rounded away. It is also now the same size as the inversion this cycle just ruled on, which is the honest reason the verdict is stated as narrowly as it is. THE CORRECTION STILL COVERS TWO OPS. aten::add at [32768,1024] reads 943.3 and aten::add_ 956.5 -- both below the reference and both on the generic model, so the generic charge is not obviously inflated. But nothing here audits the remaining ops, and the sub-1MB bucket at 28.1 GB/s shows the model can be wildly wrong in the other direction when launch cost dominates.

The elementwise class splits into a launch bound tail and a streaming bodydiagnostic, existing captureMECHANISM: the class contains a LAUNCH-BOUND TAIL whose device time is set by per-kernel cost rather than by traffic, and a STREAMING BODY whose time is set by bytes.The frozen criterion reads: REFUTED IF the elementwise class device time UNEXPLAINED BY ITS CORRECTED BYTES at the 1008 GB/s published reference is AT OR ABOVE 21,886.7 us per step, that threshold being derived from the bar before the run rather than chosen…SUPPORTED7,017 us is a real lever and it is the largest single unmeasured-treatment target left on the non-GEMM path; what it is not is 27,500 us.
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
THE THRESHOLD IS DERIVED FROM THE BAR, NOT CHOSEN AFTER THE FACT. The governed step is 0.16376 s; 1.5x needs it at 0.109173 s, a removal of 54,586.7 us. Cycle 31 stack pairs the elementwise lever with all device idle at 32,700 us, so the elementwise half must supply at least 21,886.7 us for the only above-bar arm to still clear the bar once it is sized by mechanism instead of by class size. REFUTED IF the elementwise class device time UNEXPLAINED BY ITS CORRECTED BYTES at the 1008 GB/s published reference, on trace cia-captured-trace-20260914T183257Z.json, is AT OR ABOVE 21,886.7 us per step. That would mean roughly four fifths of the class is overhead, there is no material streaming body to speak of, the class size was a fair price for the lever, and the arm still clears the bar.
Instrument

Carries the same MIN_COVERAGE 0.50 refusal, proved with a deliberate red against the no-record_shapes capture BEFORE the reading is taken -- and here the refusal matters more than in its predecessors, because a thin sized population would make every kernel fully unexplained and report the entire class as collectable, which is the maximally favourable answer.
Evidence
state/issue1945-receipts/elementwise-launch-overhead-20260915T0449Z.json
Result in full
The frozen criterion reads: REFUTED IF the elementwise class device time UNEXPLAINED BY ITS CORRECTED BYTES at the 1008 GB/s published reference is AT OR ABOVE 21,886.7 us per step, that threshold being derived from the bar before the run rather than chosen after it. The measured figure is 7,017.0 us per step, 25.6 percent of the class, and 6,906.9 us counting only sized kernels. Far below the threshold, so the criterion is not met and the hypothesis is SUPPORTED: the class does split. THE SPLIT IS SHARP AND IT IS THE SHAPE PREDICTED. Overhead-dominated kernels -- those spending more than half their device time not moving their own bytes -- number 2,086 per step, carry 8,836.7 us and achieve 252.7 GB/s. The bytes-dominated remainder is 1,361 kernels, 18,610.7 us, 1457.0 GB/s. Two populations, a factor of 5.8 apart in achieved rate, inside one class that was being priced as a single 27,500 us lever. THE CONSEQUENCE FOR THE CAMPAIGN, and it is the reason this cycle was run rather than filed. Cycle 31 only above-bar arm pairs all device idle at 32,700 us with the elementwise class at its CLASS SIZE. Substituting the mechanism-sized ceiling gives a stack of 39,717.0 us, a step of 0.124043 s, and a factor of 1.3202 -- BELOW the operator 1.5x arm bar. The instrument own contention note makes that an over-estimate rather than an under-estimate, because removing per-kernel overhead also removes some of the inter-kernel gaps already counted in device idle, and the stack figure does not net that out. SO THERE IS NOW NO MEASURED ARM ABOVE THE BAR. THE MECHANISM PREMISE IS WHERE THIS COULD BE WRONG AND IT IS NAMED RATHER THAN BURIED: the ceiling presumes a fusion treatment removes per-kernel overhead and NO bytes. A treatment that also eliminated intermediate buffers would remove traffic too and could exceed 7,017 us. That is a claim about what a treatment DOES, it is not measured here, and it is the half of a ceiling my own record says I get wrong.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THIS DOES NOT CLOSE. IT DOES NOT REFUSE FUSION AND IT MUST NOT BE QUOTED AS DOING SO. 7,017 us is a real lever and it is the largest single unmeasured-treatment target left on the non-GEMM path; what it is not is 27,500 us. Row 1 not_closed line (a) -- fusing the elementwise population to remove DEVICE TIME rather than gaps -- is now SIZED rather than closed, and its other three not_closed lines are untouched: a different geometry, the same treatment stacked behind a device-time treatment, and the behaviour under a real CUDA graph, which this path never exercises because it launches zero cudaGraphLaunch. THE BYTE-ELIMINATING VARIANT IS UNPRICED AND IS THE OBVIOUS WAY PAST THIS CEILING. The four largest unexplained sites are copy_ and add_ at [3072,1024] and [1024,3072], 6,433.2 us of device time carrying 4,273.6 us of excess at 308-349 GB/s. Those are intermediate materialise and accumulate pairs; a fusion that removes the intermediate removes bytes as well as launches and is outside this ceiling by construction. Sizing it needs the consumer read, not this trace. THE 192-CALL ROUTING TAIL IS PURE OVERHEAD AND IS SEPARATELY ACTIONABLE. gather, scatter_add_, mul and fill_ on one- and two-element tensors, 192 calls per step each, 919.8 us combined at a measured 0.0 GB/s. Nothing about that time is traffic. It is small, it is unambiguous, and it is the cleanest possible target for the launch-count reshape. IT MEASURES NO BYTES AND NO OCCUPANCY. The byte figures are counts from recorded input dims, not device traffic; this build carries neither counter. "Unexplained by bytes" is not "launch time" -- a kernel can be slow for reasons this instrument cannot name, and the 20-100 us band holding 4,466.7 us of the excess at 817.6 GB/s is exactly where that ambiguity lives. THE REFERENCE IS A PUBLISHED SPECIFICATION FIGURE used to express a fraction. Nothing here is an impossibility claim about the 80,000 terminal, which is unchanged; measurement selects the ROUTE and never the exit, and impossibility is the operator to rule from receipts. WHAT FOLLOWS IS A SELECTION AND IT IS PARTLY HIS. With no measured arm above the bar, the honest next act is to price a route nothing has measured rather than to build a lever the receipts now size at 1.32x. Successor named: FP16 accumulation, which carries the same published dense rate on Ada, which this campaign has never measured, and which I have previously and wrongly described as unavailable. The two routes I cannot price alone are fewer FLOPs per position and positions per optimizer step -- the second is a CONTRACT change and is his decision, not mine, and it is raised rather than deferred. ONE CAPTURE, ONE PROCESS, UNDER PROFILING INSTRUMENTATION, NO MATCHED-RUN VARIATION.

Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
THE THRESHOLD IS THE SAME ONE CYCLE 34 DERIVED FROM THE BAR BEFORE ITS RUN, unchanged and not re-fitted. Governed step 0.16376 s; 1.5x needs 0.109173 s, a removal of 54,586.7 us; all device idle supplies 32,700.0; so the elementwise half must supply 21,886.7 us. Cycle 34 measured the launch-only reading of that half at 7,017.0 and the arm fell to 1.3202x. Instrument
Analysis-only, no card, no lease, no new capture, claim --no-gpu. Evidence
state/issue1945-receipts/byte-reduction-ceiling-20260915T0504Z.json
Result in full
SUPPORTED, and NARROWLY, which is stated first because the margin is the finding. THE FROZEN CRITERION: "REFUTED IF the combined per-kernel capped removal -- overhead excess plus eliminable byte time, capped at each kernel measured duration -- is AT OR ABOVE 21,886.7 us per step". MEASURED: 19,105.6 us. Below the threshold, so not refuted, but by 2,781.1 us -- 12.7 percent of the contribution the bar needs. The arm reaches 1.4627x against a 1.5x bar. THE TWO HALVES, AND THE DOUBLE COUNT THE CAP REMOVED. Launch overhead 7,017.0 us (cycle 34, recomputed here and identical, which is the check that the two instruments agree). Eliminable byte time 13,703.8 us -- 47.1 percent of the class corrected bytes. Their naive sum is 20,720.8; the per-kernel cap removes 1,615.2 us of double counting and gives 19,105.6. Summing the two class totals would have credited the same microseconds twice for the 1,172.3 kernels per step the treatment removes outright, and would have reported 20,720.8 -- still below the threshold, so the cap does not change the verdict, but it changes the number and it is published so the arithmetic is checkable rather than asserted. THE CLEAN SUB-LEVER, which is the most actionable thing here. Pure movement -- copy_, fill_, zero_, clone, contiguous, _to_copy -- is 784 kernels per step carrying 7,131.1 us at 775.9 GB/s, and it is removable IN FULL by construction, because those kernels compute nothing. The arithmetic remainder is 2,663 kernels, 20,316.2 us at 1172.3 GB/s, of which 11,974.4 us is removable under maximal fusion. WHY SUPPORTED IS THE RIGHT VERDICT AND NOT INCONCLUSIVE, given how close it is. The criterion was frozen before the run and the measurement is on the favourable side of every judgement call in the instrument: MAXIMAL fusion with every intermediate assumed fusible, every pure movement op assumed absorbable, and the receipt own contention note says the stack figure is an OVER-estimate because removing per-kernel overhead also removes inter-kernel gaps already counted in the 32,700 us idle lever. The true figure is below 1.4627x, not above it. A run that lands below its threshold on the most generous possible reading discriminated; it was not inconclusive.
Limitations, and what this does not close
WHAT THE REFUTATION DID NOT CLOSE, and the 2,781.1 us margin makes this the sharpest opening the campaign has had in days rather than a footnote. The stack priced here is exactly TWO levers: all device idle (32,700) and maximal elementwise fusion (19,105.6). It clears the bar if ANY third distinct lever supplies 2,781.1 us of the step. That is 1.7 percent of the governed step. NOT CLOSED (a) THE OPTIMIZER, which is the successor. A registry not_closed line already says it verbatim -- "THE OPTIMIZER IS 16.4 ms, 12.4 percent of the step, and nothing in this campaign has examined it as a route. Its bytes are unsized here because _fused_adamw_ takes TensorLists whose dims the profiler records empty -- sizing needs the parameter/state inventory, not the trace." The margin needed is 2,781.1 of roughly 16,400 us: 17 percent of a class nobody has measured. It is also the one class whose bytes are computable WITHOUT the trace, from the parameter and optimizer-state inventory, so it is analysis-only work. NOT CLOSED (b) fusion as an ENABLER OF CONCURRENCY by reducing dependency edges. Every instrument in this sequence prices REMOVAL; none prices what a shorter dependency chain does to overlap, and the overlap row is still INCONCLUSIVE with all its own not_closed lines. NOT CLOSED (c) the pure-movement sub-lever as its own arm. 7,131.1 us at 784 kernels is removable by construction and does not need the fusible-intermediate premise that the other 11,974.4 us rests on. It is the highest-confidence slice of this ceiling and it has never been separated out as a treatment. NOT CLOSED (d) the mechanism premise itself. Nothing here shows any particular intermediate is fusible with its neighbour; register and shared-memory capacity, the residual stream, recompute boundaries and the autograd tape all force some back to memory, and a real implementation reaches less than 19,105.6. So the honest reading is that the elementwise class plus all idle does NOT reach 1.5x, and the third lever is required rather than optional. NOT CLOSED (e) positions per optimizer step, which is a CONTRACT change and the operator call, already raised to him and still his.

the-optimizer-is-the-unexamined-lever-that-decides-the-1_5x-arm-20260915
Record
the-byte-reduction-half-of-elementwise-fusion-is-also-below-the-bar-20260915 · 2026-09-15T05:02:32Z → 2026-09-15T05:05:22Z · full entry
The optimizer is the unexamined lever that decides the 1 5x armdiagnostic, existing captureTHE MECHANISM, named as a thing that is either true or false rather than as a restatement: the optimizer class is BANDWIDTH-BOUND OVER ITS OWN STATE FOOTPRINT, and its roughly 16,400 us per step is…The frozen criterion reads: "REFUTED IF the optimizer class REMOVABLE time -- measured optimizer device time per step MINUS the bandwidth floor implied by its own state footprint -- is BELOW 2,781.1 us per step." Removable is 10,234.0 us per step, so the…SUPPORTEDWHICH SUB-MECHANISM CARRIES THE EXCESS -- the criterion required this, and the honest answer is partial, so it is given as one measured term, one ELIMINATED candidate, and one open remainder.
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
REFUTED IF the optimizer class REMOVABLE time -- measured optimizer device time per step MINUS the bandwidth floor implied by its own state footprint -- is BELOW 2,781.1 us per step. THE THRESHOLD IS DERIVED FROM THE BAR AND NOT FROM THE DATA, and it is fixed here before any number is read: governed step 0.16376 s; 1.5x needs the step at 0.109173 s, a removal of 54,586.7 us; the two levers already priced supply all-device-idle 32,700.0 us and maximal elementwise fusion 19,105.6 us, capped composite 51,805.6; the shortfall is 54,586.7 - 51,805.6 = 2,781.1 us. That is exactly what a third lever must supply, so it is exactly the bar this class must clear to be the lever that decides the arm. THE FLOOR IS THE HONEST, MOST HOSTILE FORM OF THE COMPARISON. necessary_bytes = sum over the four AdamW tensors of trainable_elements x dtype_size, times the MINIMUM traversal count a correct fused AdamW must perform (read p, grad, exp_avg, exp_avg_sq; write p, exp_avg, exp_avg_sq), and floor_us = necessary_bytes / (1008 GB/s) x 1e6 -- 1008 being the PUBLISHED specification used to express a fraction, never a measured ceiling and never an impossibility premise. Using the published rate makes the floor as LOW as it can be and therefore the removable excess as LARGE as it can be, so the criterion is biased toward SUPPORTING the hypothesis; a refutation under it is a real refutation. SUPPORTED IF removable >= 2,781.1 us -- and the update must then say which sub-mechanism carries the excess (redundant traversals, an unfused path, state wider than the update needs, or a separate gradient-clearing pass), because "there is excess" names no treatment. INCONCLUSIVE IS RESERVED, and narrowly: only if the optimizer device kernels cannot be identified in the timeline at all, or the trainable-parameter footprint cannot be read from the inventory. A number that lands just under 2,781.1 is a REFUTATION, not an inconclusive result, and cycle 35 already proved the reverse case -- 19,105.6 against 21,886.7, ruled SUPPORTED at a 12.7 percent margin rather than softened. The same discipline binds in this direction.
Instrument
ANALYSIS-ONLY, no card, no lease, no new capture -- and deliberately sourcing the two halves from two places, NEITHER of which is the blind one. The minimum correct fused AdamW traversal -- read param, grad, exp_avg, exp_avg_sq; write param and both moments -- is therefore 6,636,421,120 bytes, and at the PUBLISHED 1008 GB/s (a specification used to express a fraction, never a measured ceiling for this card and never an impossibility premise) the floor is 6,583.8 us/step. 16,817.8 - 6,583.8 = 10,234.0 us, which is 3.68x the 2,781.1 us the bar needs from a third lever. THE CRITERION WAS BIASED TOWARD THIS ANSWER AND THE RESULT SURVIVES REMOVING THE BIAS. The published rate makes the floor lowest and the excess largest, so support under it is the weakest form of support. With no counterpart seat to issue a PASS, that deliberate red and the execution of the real consumer are what this verdict rests on. WHAT THIS VERDICT IS NOT. Removable time is the distance between a measurement and a floor. It is not a ceiling on any treatment, not a claim that any mechanism collects it, and not an assertion about what this card can do. No arm is named by this ruling.

WHAT THE REFUTATION DID NOT CLOSE, and what this SUPPORT does not open. A SUPPORTED verdict here closes nothing about routes either: it says the optimizer CONTAINS the 2,781.1 us shortfall with room to spare, not that any mechanism collects it. The three levers now priced (idle 32,700.0, elementwise 19,105.6, optimizer 10,234.0) act on different milliseconds only if they do not contend, and contention has NOT been checked -- the optimizer runs strictly after backward on one stream, which is precisely the serialization the idle lever claims, so these two may be pricing overlapping milliseconds and summing them would be the contending-ceilings error. That check is the successor and it outranks naming a treatment. Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
REFUTED IF the intersection of the elementwise and optimizer kernel sets, measured over the same six census windows by the two selectors VERBATIM as their own levers wrote them, is NONZERO -- specifically, if the pro-rated double-counted time exceeds 1.0 us per step. THE THRESHOLD IS SET BEFORE THE NUMBER IS READ and it is deliberately near zero, because the claim under test is DISJOINTNESS and disjointness has no tolerance band: one shared kernel family means the stack was built by double counting, whatever its size. A nonzero intersection is a REFUTATION of the disjointness mechanism even if the corrected stack still clears the bar; the verdict is about the composition being sound, not about the bar being met. SUPPORTED IF the intersection is zero or below 1.0 us per step AND the re-derived timing check reproduces zero concurrency (sum of kernel durations equal to their interval union within 1.0 us per step). Both halves are required: the registry row that measured concurrency predates both levers, and a not_closed line is a claim about the past, so it is re-derived here rather than inherited. THE INSTRUMENT MUST REFUSE AN EMPTY SET RATHER THAN RETURN ZERO. An empty selector makes the intersection trivially zero, which would read as SUPPORT -- "the levers are disjoint" -- when it actually means the selector matched nothing. Opposite in meaning, identical in arithmetic. The script exits 2 on an empty lever set and --prove-red blanks the elementwise selector to prove that path fires before the green run. INCONCLUSIVE IS RESERVED, and narrowly: only if the trace carries no census_step windows, or if both selectors match nothing at all. A small nonzero intersection is a REFUTATION, not an inconclusive result. WHAT NO VERDICT HERE MAY CLAIM: neither outcome prices a treatment, names an arm, or establishes that the three removals are independent actions. Disjoint populations bound DOUBLE COUNTING only.

The governed-run evidence contract separation clause forbids exactly this composition, and the 32,700.0 us idle lever does NOT come from this number -- it comes from cycle 34 on a governed basis. A fused elementwise path changes which kernels the optimizer waits behind, and a shorter kernel sequence changes how much device idle exists at all -- so the idle lever and the two kernel levers may be non-additive in the opposite direction from double counting: removing kernel time could consume some of the same idle the idle lever claims, because idle is the complement of busy and shrinking busy does not shrink the span. That is the successor. (3) Each lever individually remains a CEILING under maximal effectiveness, not a measured effect: idle assumes every hole closes, elementwise assumes every intermediate stays in registers, and the optimizer figure is distance to a bandwidth floor under a published rate. Three perfect-effectiveness ceilings summing to 1.137x of the requirement is a thin result, and saying so is the point of this line. (4) One profiled trace, one geometry, no matched-run variation, so nothing here supports an improvement claim. (5) Both selectors are inherited verbatim from their own levers; if either mis-scopes its class, this census inherits that error exactly, and the 0.1 us agreement only validates the elementwise one.

The frozen criterion set three outcomes: REFUTED if the UNION hostile figure under RETIRES-NOTHING exceeds 7,452.9 us/step; SUPPORTED if the UNION hostile figure under RETIRES-EVERYTHING is at or below it; INCONCLUSIVE on a straddle. RETIRES-NOTHING (what the optimizer receipt literally prices, since 10,234.0 us is measured 16,817.8 minus a 6,583.8 us bandwidth floor -- kernels shrunk, not launches retired): union hostile share 0.2276 -> 7,441.8 us, stack 54,597.8, CLEARS by 11.2 us. This reproduces cycle 38 to the decimal, which is the internal consistency check the instrument was built to give for free. RETIRES-EVERYTHING (the extreme bound, every optimizer launch gone): union hostile share 0.2338 -> 7,646.9 us, stack 54,392.7, DOES NOT CLEAR, short by 193.9 us. SO THE FINDING IS A DEPENDENCY, NOT A NUMBER: whether the three-lever stack reaches 1.5x under the hostile reading depends on the SHAPE of the optimizer treatment, a quantity nobody has specified. A bandwidth-floor optimizer that only widens each access retires no launches and the stack holds by 11.2 us; one that fuses the 963.9 us separate gradient-clearing pass retires launches and it can go short by up to 193.9 us. That dependency has to be resolved before any three-lever arm is authored, and it could not have been seen from either lever alone. NOT EVERY READING STRADDLES, and saying so is part of the verdict: under the CONSERVATIVE measure the stack clears in BOTH scenarios, by 2,300.4 us and 2,095.2 us. The straddle is specific to the hostile arm of the criterion, and see the update for why that arm is loose by construction. THE REFUSAL WAS PROVED BEFORE THE GREEN RUN, and this cycle is the one where it mattered most: --prove-red blanks the optimizer selector and the script exits 2, because "the optimizer retires no gaps" is this cycle's own RETIRES-NOTHING result and "the selector matched nothing" is a defect -- identical in arithmetic, and unusually easy to conflate here precisely because one of them is a result the cycle expected to see.
Limitations, and what this does not close
A STRUCTURAL FACT IN THIS CYCLE'S OWN OUTPUT SHOWS THE HOSTILE ARM OF MY CRITERION IS LOOSE BY CONSTRUCTION, and it is confirmed rather than argued. The census reports 7,438.7 kernels per step and 7,437.7 gaps per step -- EXACTLY kernels minus one. So the in-window timeline is a single unbroken serialized chain with no coincident kernels and no zero-length gaps, which the instrument would have dropped. On such a chain, removing R interior kernels leaves K-R kernels and therefore K-R-1 gaps: EXACTLY R gaps disappear, never "every gap adjacent to a removed kernel". Removing one of a gap's two bounding kernels MERGES that gap with its neighbour instead of deleting it -- cycle 38 said this in its own docstring and then still froze a criterion on the adjacency count. The arithmetic confirms the identity: the conservative share 0.1576 equals 1,172.3 removed kernels over 7,437.7 gaps, 0.15762. The CONSERVATIVE measure is not a cautious lower bound at all -- it IS the structurally correct count, and the hostile measure is an artifact of counting adjacency instead of retirement. Under the correct measure contested idle is 5,152.6 us (RETIRES-NOTHING) or 5,357.7 us (RETIRES-EVERYTHING) and the stack clears by 2,300.4 or 2,095.2 us in both. THE VERDICT STAYS INCONCLUSIVE ANYWAY, and that is deliberate. The criterion was frozen before the numbers and binds on the hostile arm; re-reading it now against a derivation I made after seeing the result is exactly the move this loop exists to prevent, and it would be the move that converts an unwelcome verdict into a welcome one. The derivation instead becomes the SUCCESSOR, where it is frozen as the criterion BEFORE the run and the instrument is changed to measure retirement rather than adjacency. What that successor cannot do is claim novelty: the answer is already visible here, so it is scoped to confirming the identity on a second geometry and re-ruling the composition on the correct measure, not to rediscovering it. WHAT REMAINS OPEN. (1) The optimizer retirement fraction is still BOUNDED 0.0 to 1.0 and unmeasured, and resolving it needs the treatment SPECIFIED, not another trace -- this is now the binding prerequisite on any three-lever arm. (2) Applying a count share to a duration total still assumes contested gaps are of average duration; the correct-measure figure inherits that assumption exactly, and this trace cannot check it because its durations are instrumentation-dominated. (3) The BOTH-adjacent term used an independent-retirement reading, the largest lawful union; at 3.0 gaps per step it changes nothing either way. (4) All three levers remain MAXIMAL-EFFECTIVENESS ceilings rather than measured effects, so even a comfortably clearing corrected stack licenses an arm on ceilings. (5) One profiled trace, one geometry, no matched-run variation.
