does-the-optimizer-lever-contest-the-idle-lever-gaps-too-20260915 · 2026-09-15T06:17:52Z → 2026-09-15T06:19:46Z · full entry
Retired gaps equal retired kernels so re rule the stack on the correct measurediagnostic, existing captureIf the in-window device timeline is a single unbroken serialized chain -- no coincident kernels, no zero-length gaps -- then K kernels have exactly K-1 gaps.The frozen criterion required BOTH that the chain identity hold in every window of every source AND that the RETIRES-EVERYTHING figure be at or below 7,452.9 us/step.SUPPORTEDThe criterion says "every window of every GEOMETRY supplied".
Methods, evidence and limitations
Criterion, frozen before the run
REFUTED IF the contested-idle figure on the RETIREMENT measure under the RETIRES-NOTHING scenario EXCEEDS 7,452.9 us per step -- the composition fails even under the reading most favourable to it. ALSO REFUTED, independently of any share, IF THE CHAIN IDENTITY FAILS in any window of any geometry supplied: the retirement argument requires the single serialized chain, so an identity failure refutes the correction itself rather than the composition, and the instrument exits non-zero rather than publishing a share. The threshold is unchanged from cycles 38 and 39 and derives from the bar, not from anything read here: 1.5x on the 0.16376 s governed step needs 54,586.7 us removed, the naive three-lever stack is 62,039.6 us, margin exactly 7,452.9 us.
Instrument
scripts/issue1945/retired_gap_recount.py over BOTH captured traces (cia-captured-trace-20260914T183257Z.json and cia-captured-trace-20260914T182441Z.json). Selectors copied verbatim from the levers that priced them. Deliberate red PROVED BEFORE the green run: --prove-red injects a coincident kernel into every window so the chain identity must fail, and the script exits 2 naming the offending window rather than publishing a share -- because an identity failure would make every number below wrong in a way that looks perfectly reasonable, which is worse than a refusal. Analysis-only: reads traces already on disk, opens no GPU window, claimed --no-gpu.
Evidence
state/issue1945-receipts/retired-gap-recount-20260915T0710Z.json
Result in full
The frozen criterion required BOTH that the chain identity hold in every window of every source AND that the RETIRES-EVERYTHING figure be at or below 7,452.9 us/step. Both hold, so SUPPORTED. CHAIN IDENTITY: 7,438.7 kernels/step and 7,437.7 gaps/step, gaps == kernels-1 in all 6 windows of each source, 12 of 12. The in-window device timeline is a single unbroken serialized chain, which is what the retirement argument requires and what cycle 37 independently established by a different route (sum of kernel durations equals their interval union). RE-RULED ON THE RETIREMENT MEASURE: retiring 1,172.3 elementwise kernels retires exactly 1,172.3 gaps of 7,437.7, a share of 0.1576 -> 5,154.1 us contested, stack 56,885.5, clears by 2,298.9 us. Adding all 46.7 optimizer kernels (RETIRES-EVERYTHING) gives 1,219.0 gaps, 0.1639 -> 5,359.2 us, stack 56,680.4, clears by 2,093.7 us. SO CYCLE 39'S STRADDLE WAS AN ARTIFACT OF THE ADJACENCY MEASURE, and this is the substantive result. Under the corrected measure the composition clears under BOTH optimizer scenarios, which RETIRES the "binding prerequisite" cycle 39 named: the 1.5x stack does NOT depend on the shape of the optimizer treatment. It also raises cycle 38's margin from 11.2 us to 2,093.7 us in the worst case -- the knife-edge was my measure, not the timeline. Both prior verdicts stand as ruled, because both were frozen before their numbers; what changes is the figure they were ruling on, and the correction runs AGAINST the direction that would have flattered me at the time, since a loose hostile bound is what made cycle 38 look dramatic. THE REFUSAL WAS PROVED BEFORE THE GREEN RUN: --prove-red injects a coincident kernel into every window and the script exits 2 naming the first offender (window 3, 7,440 kernels, 7,438 gaps) rather than publishing a share -- because an identity failure makes every downstream number wrong in a way that looks perfectly reasonable, which is worse than a refusal.
Limitations, and what this does not close
A DEFECT IN MY OWN CRITERION, DISCLOSED BECAUSE IT NARROWS WHAT THIS SUPPORTS. The criterion says "every window of every GEOMETRY supplied". What I supplied were two distinct CAPTURES of the same geometry. The files are genuinely different -- 149,625,239 bytes / sha256 15bc65df... and 113,277,936 bytes / sha256 c78c7f08... -- and their in-window device-kernel structure is identical to the decimal: 7,438.7 kernels, 7,437.7 gaps, 3,447.0 elementwise, 46.7 optimizer per step in both. That identity across two independent captures is exactly what a deterministic captured step should produce and it is real corroboration of the STRUCTURE. It is NOT a second geometry and it is NOT matched-run variation, so it supports no improvement or ceiling claim, and the governed-run evidence contract's repeatability clause remains unsatisfied for anything this stack is later used to claim. I checked the digests specifically because two sources agreeing to four significant figures on every field is the shape of reading the same file twice; they are different files, and the agreement is a finding rather than an artifact. WHAT THIS CORRECTION DOES AND DOES NOT REACH. It fixes the MEASURE of gap contention and nothing else. It does not make any lever a measured effect, it does not change the 32,700.0 us idle lever's basis, and it does not touch the assumption that most needs attention next. THE LARGEST REMAINING UNVERIFIED ASSUMPTION IS NOW EXPLICIT AND UNCHANGED BY THIS CYCLE: the share is a COUNT applied to a DURATION total, which assumes retired gaps are of average duration. If the kernels the elementwise lever removes sit in denser launch clusters than average -- which is plausible, since clustered small kernels are exactly what fusion targets -- their gaps are shorter than average and the contention is SMALLER than 5,154.1 us; if they are isolated, it is larger. Neither trace can settle it, because settling it needs gap DURATIONS and these are instrumentation-dominated at ~503,890 us per window against a 163,760 us governed step. That is a governed-basis measurement, not a re-analysis, and it is the honest successor. ALSO STILL OPEN: (1) all three levers remain MAXIMAL-EFFECTIVENESS ceilings rather than measured effects, so the arm this stack licenses under 1945-multiplicative-movement-only clause 1 is licensed on ceilings, and that is now the binding weakness rather than the contention. (2) The optimizer retirement count stays bounded 0.0 to 46.7 per step; this cycle made that question MOOT for the 1.5x verdict rather than answering it. (3) No matched-run variation exists for any of it.

Limitations of this account

It is not the complete research history: a selected subset of receipts is published and the rest are referenced by name. It records no new verdicts, edits no receipt, and holds no acceptance criterion. Where a later finding changes how an earlier result should be read, it is carried as a dated annotation beside that result — the original verdict is never rewritten. Technical notes and the generator itself are published under Agents.
